%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000004545000160  
%            ALGOL ACCESS TO POSIX PROCEDURES                           0000004645000180  
%                                                                       0000004745000200  
% This ALGOL include file provides POSIX library procedure              0000004845000220  
% declarations for the POSIX MCPSUPPORT library interfaces.  This file  0000004945000240  
% can be included in ALGOL programs.  Programmers can access the new    0000005045000260  
% procedures using the names defined in this file instead of the actual 0000005145000280  
% names.  The names are chosen to mimic the "C" POSIX API calls.        0000005245000300  
% Additional symbols are defined to give more flexibility to the user   0000005345000320  
% for setting flags or values.                                          0000005445000340  
%                                                                       0000005545000360  
%  This file is organized in the following fashion:                     0000005645000380  
%                                                                       0000005745000400  
%  o  Part 1 of the file contains the interface declarations and        0000005845000420  
%     selector values                                                   0000005945000430  
%     1.   signal related calls                                         0000006045000440  
%     2.   semaphore related calls                                      0000006145000460  
%     3.   user/group info related calls                                0000006245000480  
%     4.   selector values are defined to symbols                       0000006345000490  
%                                                                       0000006445000500  
%  o  Part 2 of the file contains the macro definitions                 0000006545000520  
%     1.   errno values are defined to symbols                          0000006645000540  
%     2.   other flags are defined to symbols                           0000006745000580  
%                                                                       0000006845000600  
%  o  Part 3 of the file contains the re-definition of procedure        0000006945000620  
%     calls to simulate "C" interface call names.                       0000007045000640  
%                                                                       0000007145000660  
%  It is recommended that user access to these procedures be            0000007245000680  
%  made using the constants in PART 2 and the interfaces in PART 3.     0000007345000700  
%                                                                       0000007445000720  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000007545000740  
%                                                                       0000007645000760  
%                                                                       0000007745000780  
%  MCPSUPPORT Library Interfaces for POSIX                              0000007845000800  
%                                                                       0000007945000820  
%  General Definition Rules                                             0000008045000840  
%  ========================                                             0000008145000860  
%  1. All MCP interfaces which take pointers to character strings or    0000008245000880  
%     structures as input parameters will also take a by-value integer  0000008345000900  
%     string or structure length as a separate parameter.  The appli-   0000008445000920  
%     cation is responsible for providing this length. The MCP routines 0000008545000940  
%     will rely on this length information to determine how much of the 0000008645000960  
%     string or structure information is valid.                         0000008745000980  
%                                                                       0000008845001000  
%     In the case of strings, if the length is -1, the called function  0000008945001020  
%     will assume that the string is NULL terminated, and act according-0000009045001040  
%     ly.  If a string length is provided, it is assumed to NOT include 0000009145001060  
%     any trailing NULL.                                                0000009245001080  
%                                                                       0000009345001100  
%     All input structures with embedded strings will include an integer0000009445001120  
%     preceding the start of the string.  This integer will contain the 0000009545001140  
%     length of the string. If the length is -1, the MCP will assume    0000009645001160  
%     that the string is NULL terminated; if the length is not -1, it   0000009745001180  
%     will be taken as the length of the string,  not including the     0000009845001200  
%     NULL.                                                             0000009945001220  
%                                                                       0000010045001240  
%  2. All MCP interfaces which take pointers to character strings or    0000010145001260  
%     structures as output parameters will also return the length of the0000010245001280  
%     string or structure in a by-reference integer output parameter    0000010345001300  
%     unless such information can be returned as the interface result.  0000010445001320  
%     (See 5. for more details.)                                        0000010545001340  
%     Additionally, a by-value integer maximum string or structure      0000010645001360  
%     length input parameter must be provided.  The MCP interfaces      0000010745001380  
%     require a valid string or structure length maximum value, since   0000010845001400  
%     this length in conjunction with the actual size of the string or  0000010945001420  
%     structure container will determine how much of the string or      0000011045001440  
%     structure information will be written.                            0000011145001460  
%                                                                       0000011245001480  
%     The MCP interfaces will place a terminating NULL at the end of an 0000011345001500  
%     output character string if there is room and the returned length  0000011445001520  
%     will not include the NULL character.                              0000011545001540  
%                                                                       0000011645001560  
%     No error will be returned if there is no space for a NULL         0000011745001580  
%     character at the end of the data. However if there is insufficient0000011845001600  
%     room for the data, then EINVAL will be returned in the            0000011945001620  
%     by-reference integer output parameter ERRNO and unexpected result 0000012045001640  
%     will be returned. The documentation for each interface specifies  0000012145001660  
%     the behavior for all possible conditions.                         0000012245001700  
%                                                                       0000012345001720  
%     All output structures with embedded strings will include an       0000012445001740  
%     integer preceding the start of the string.  This integer will     0000012545001760  
%     contain the length of the string, excluding the NULL; overflow    0000012645001780  
%     conditions will be handled as described above.                    0000012745001800  
%                                                                       0000012845001820  
%  3. The application is responsible for guaranteeing the alignment of  0000012945001840  
%     embedded word numeric types (i.e.  integers) within structures for0000013045001860  
%     all MCP interfaces which take pointers to structures.  All        0000013145001880  
%     structures must start on a word boundary.                         0000013245001900  
%                                                                       0000013345001920  
%  4. For MCP interfaces with Selector parameters, any parameter (input 0000013445001940  
%     or output) is undefined if not used for that selector value. The  0000013545001960  
%     documentation for each interface states which parameters are      0000013645001980  
%     valid and which are undefined for each selector value.            0000013745002000  
%                                                                       0000013845002020  
%  5. All MCP interfaces return an integer or real result.  The result, 0000013945002040  
%     -1, indicates that an error has occurred and that ERRNO has       0000014045002060  
%     probably been modified.  A meaningful positive or negative result 0000014145002080  
%     may also be returned if specified by POSIX. MCP interfaces        0000014245002100  
%     never return a pointer result; however, if the POSIX              0000014345002120  
%     interface either returns a pointer, or an output pointer is its   0000014445002140  
%     only output parameter AND the function returns no other meaningful0000014545002160  
%     result, then the results of such functions will be the length of  0000014645002180  
%     the string supplied by the MCP.                                   0000014745002200  
%                                                                       0000014845002220  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000014945002240  
$$PAGE                                                                  0000015045003000  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                                   0000015145003020  
%    PART 1         %                                                   0000015245003040  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                                   0000015345003060

% POSIX_FCNTL CMD values    %                                           0000143645607460  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                           0000143745607480  
% CMD values and associated parameters are as follows:                  0000143845607500  
   F_DUPFD  = 0 #, % Only supported for POSIX Global Files.  Causes file0000143945607520  
                   % descriptor to be copied into the first available   0000144045607540  
                   % file descriptor vector table entry.                0000144145607560  
   F_GETFD  = 1 #, % Returns current value of the file descriptor flags.0000144245607580  
   F_SETFD  = 2 #, % Sets the file descriptor flags.                    0000144345607600  
   F_GETFL  = 3 #, % Returns current value of the file status flags.    0000144445607620  
   F_SETFL  = 4 #, % Sets the file status flags.                        0000144545607640  
   F_GETLK  = 5 #, % Get the first lock which blocks the lock described 0000144645607660  
                   % by the flock structure.                            0000144745607680  
   F_SETLK  = 6 #, % An attempt is made to set or clear the lock        0000144845607700  
                   % specified by the flock structure.                  0000144945607720  
   F_SETLKW = 7 #; %Same as F_SETLK except when a blocking lock is found0000145045607740  
                                                                        0000145145607760  
DEFINE                                                                  0000145245607780  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%    0000145345607800  
%       POSIX flock STRUCTURE (POSIX_FCNTL)                        %    0000145445607820  
%..................................................................%    0000145545607840  
% The flock structure is used to specify locks for POSIX defined   %    0000145645607860  
% advisory locking.  Advisory locking is supported by POSIX_FCNTL. %    0000145745607880  
% POSIX_FCNTL uses FILELOCKER which also provides record locking   %    0000145845607900  
% for general A Series use.  The words in the flock structure map  %    0000145945607920  
% to FILELOCKER pararmeters.                                       %    0000146045607940  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%    0000146145607960  
    %-Word-offset-- %--Description--------------  %--FILELOCKER params  0000146245607980  
    L_TYPE   = 0 #, % Type of lock.               % TYPE                0000146345608000  
    L_WHENCE = 1 #, % Where L_START is based.     % WHENCE              0000146445608020  
    L_START  = 2 #, % Relative offset in bytes.   % START  RETURNSTART  0000146545608040  
    L_LEN    = 3 #, % Length of area in bytes.    % LEN    RETURNLEN    0000146645608060  
    L_PID    = 4 #, % Process ID of lock holder.  % n/a.   RETURNPID    0000146745608080  
    L_PAD1   = 5 #, % For future growth.          % n/a.                0000146845608100  
    FLOCK_LEN= 6 #; % Length of "flock" structure                       0000146945608120  
                                                                        0000147045608140  
   DEFINE                                                               0000147145608160  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000147245608180  
   % Type of lock (L_TYPE) %                                            0000147345608200  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000147445608220  
      F_RDLCK   =  1 #, % reader lock                                   0000147545608240  
      F_WRLCK   =  2 #, % writer lock                                   0000147645608260  
      F_UNLCK   =  3 #; % unlock (or not locked)                        0000147745608280  
                                                                        0000147845608300  
   DEFINE                                                               0000147945608320  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000148045608340  
   %      L_WHENCE         %                                            0000148145608360  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000148245608380  
      SEEK_SET  =  0 #, % apply offset from beginning of file.          0000148345608400  
      SEEK_CUR  =  1 #, % apply offset from current file pointer pos.   0000148445608420  
      SEEK_END  =  2 #; % apply offset from EOF (byte past last written)0000148545608440  
                                                                        0000148645608460  
   DEFINE                                                               0000148745608480  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000148845608500  
   % file descriptor flags %                                            0000148945608520  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000149045608540  
      FD_CLOEXECF = [0:1] #; % close the file descriptor upon execution 0000149145608560  
                             % of an exec-family functions.             0000149245608580  
                                                                        0000149345608600  
   DEFINE                                                               0000149445608620  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                          0000149545608640

DEFINE    ACCESS(PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE,                   0000178146100010  
                       PATH_SEARCHRULE, AMODE, ERRNO)     =             0000178246100020

                                  PATH_SEARCHRULE, AMODE, ERRNO) #;     0000178446100040  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000178546100050  
%   ACCESS: This procedure allows the caller to determine if the        0000178646100060  
%           permissions specified via AMODE are available for the file  0000178746100070  
%           specified by PATH.                                          0000178846100080  
%                                                                       0000178946100090  
%   INPUT:  For the description of PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE, 0000179046100100  
%           and PATH_SEARCHRULE, refer to the POSIX_ACCESS Pathname     0000179146100110  
%           defines in this file.                                       0000179246100120  
%           AMODE (integer) - Specifies Bitwise inclusive OR of the     0000179346100130  
%                 following set of permissions:                         0000179446100140  
%                                                                       0000179546100150  
%               NAME/VALUE   BIT   Permission                           0000179646100160  
%               ----------  -----  ----------                           0000179746100170  
%               R_OK = 4   [2:1]  Read                                  0000179846100180  
%               W_OK = 2   [1:1]  Write                                 0000179946100190  
%               X_OK = 1   [0:1]  Execute/Search                        0000180046100200  
%               F_OK = 0    ---   Existence (always checked)            0000180146100210  
%                                                                       0000180246100220  
%   OUTPUT: none                                                        0000180346100230  
%                                                                       0000180446100240  
%   RESULT:                                                             0000180546100250  
%     If an error occurs, ERRNO is set and a -1 result is returned.     0000180646100260  
%     Otherwise 0 is returned indicating the file exists and the        0000180746100270  
%     caller has the access permissions specified by AMODE.             0000180846100280  
%     If the specified file name is shared by both a file and a         0000180946100290  
%     directory, access will be checked for the directory.              0000181046100300  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000181146100310  
                                                                        0000181246100320  
DEFINE    ALARM(SECS, ERRNO)     =                                      0000181346200000  
            POSIX_NANOALARM(SECS, ERRNO) #;                             0000181446200020  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000181546200040  
%   ALARM: causes the system to send the calling process a SIGALARM     0000181646200060  
%          signal after a specified number of seconds elapse.           0000181746200080  
%                                                                       0000181846200100  
%   INPUT: SECS (real) - number of elapsed seconds before the signal    0000181946200120  
%                        is released.                                   0000182046200140  
%                                                                       0000182146200160  
%   OUTPUT: none                                                        0000182246200180  
%                                                                       0000182346200200  
%   RETURN: Upon successful completion, the procedure returns the number0000182446200220  
%           of seconds left in the previous alarm request. The procedure0000182546200240  
%           returns 0 if there was no previous alarm request.           0000182646200260  
%           If an error occurs, the procedure returns -1 and ERRNO      0000182746200280  
%           is set to indicate the error.                               0000182846200300  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000182946200320  
                                                                        0000183046200340  
                                                                        0000183146200360  
DEFINE    CHDIR(PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN,                               0000183246202010  
                                PATH_TYPE, PATH_SEARCHRULE, ERRNO) =    0000183346202020  
POSIX_CHANGEDIR(PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN,                               0000183446202030  
                                PATH_TYPE, PATH_SEARCHRULE, ERRNO) #;   0000183546202040  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000183646202050  
%   CHDIR: changes the current working directory                        0000183746202060  
%                                                                       0000183846202070  
%   INPUT:  For the description of PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE, 0000183946202080  
%           and PATH_SEARCHRULE, refer to the POSIX_ACCESS Pathname     0000184046202090  
%           defines in this file. SEARCHRULE_POSIX is the only valid    0000184146202100  
%           option for CHDIR function.                                  0000184246202110  
%                                                                       0000184346202120  
%   OUTPUT: none                                                        0000184446202130  
%                                                                       0000184546202140  
%   RETURN: Upon successful completion, the procedure returns 0.        0000184646202150  
%           If an error occurs, the procedure returns -1 and ERRNO is   0000184746202160  
%           set to indicate the error.                                  0000184846202170

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000191546216200  
%   EXECVE:                                                             0000191646216210  
%     This procedure handles all the user exec interfaces.              0000191746216220  
%                                                                       0000191846216230  
%   INPUT:  For the description of PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE, 0000191946216240  
%           and PATH_SEARCHRULE, refer to the POSIX_ACCESS Pathname     0000192046216250  
%           defines in this file.                                       0000192146216260  
%     ARGV - an ebcdic array containing parameters to be passed to the  0000192246216270  
%        program being execed.                                          0000192346216280  
%     ARGV_OFF (integer) - not used (only exist for consistency).       0000192446216290  
%     ARGV_LEN (integer) - not used (only exist for consistency).       0000192546216300  
%     ARGVINDX - an integer array of indexes into ARGV which point to   0000192646216310  
%        the start of each argument.                                    0000192746216320  
%     ARGVINDX_OFF (integer) - offset into ARGVINDX of where the        0000192846216330  
%        indexes start.                                                 0000192946216340  
%     ARGVINDX_LEN (integer) - number of parameters in ARGV             0000193046216350  
%        (number of indexes).                                           0000193146216360  
%     ARGVSZ - an integer array containing to length of each parameter  0000193246216370  
%        in ARGV pointed to by the index found in ARGVINDX.             0000193346216380  
%     ARGVSZ_OFF (integer) - offset into ARGVSZ of where the sizes      0000193446216390  
%        are stored.                                                    0000193546216400  
%     ARGVSZ_LEN (integer) - number of parameters in ARGV (number       0000193646216410  
%        of sizes). Must be equal to ARGVINDX_LEN.                      0000193746216420  
%     ENVP - an ebcdic array containing environment parameters to be    0000193846216430  
%        passed to the program being execed.                            0000193946216440  
%     ENVP_OFF (integer) - not used (only exist for consistency).       0000194046216450  
%     ENVP_LEN (integer) - not used (only exist for consistency).       0000194146216460  
%     ENVPINDX - an integer array of indexes into ENVP which point to   0000194246216470  
%        the start of each environment parameter.                       0000194346216480  
%     ENVPINDX_OFF (integer) - offset into ENVPINDX of where the indexes0000194446216490  
%        start.                                                         0000194546216500  
%     ENVPINDX_LEN (integer) - number of environment parameters in ENVP 0000194646216510  
%        (number of indexes).                                           0000194746216520  
%     ENVPSZ - an integer array containing to length of each environment0000194846216530  
%        parameter in ENVP pointed to by the index found in ENVPINDX.   0000194946216540  
%     ENVPSZ_OFF (integer) - offset into ENVPSZ of where the sizes are  0000195046216550  
%        stored.                                                        0000195146216560  
%     ENVPSZ_LEN (integer) - number of parameters in ENVP (number of    0000195246216570

%                                                                       0000195446216590  
%   OUTPUT: none                                                        0000195546216600  
%                                                                       0000195646216610  
%   RETURN: If this procedure returns to the calling process, an error  0000195746216620

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000250146450080  
%   SEMCTL: provides a set of semaphore control operations as           0000250246450100  
%           specified by the CMD parameter.                             0000250346450120  
%                                                                       0000250446450140  
%   INPUT: SEMID (integer) - semaphore id for a set                     0000250546450160  
%          SEMNUM (integer) - semaphore member number within a set      0000250646450180  
%          CMD (integer) - action to be performed                       0000250746450200  
%              IPC_STAT    %read semaphore values of the semaphore set  0000250846450220  
%              IPC_SET     %set semaphore values of the semaphore set   0000250946450240  
%              IPC_RMID    %remove semaphore id from the system         0000251046450260  
%              SEM_GETNCNT %get # procs waiting for a certain sem value 0000251146450280  
%              SEM_GETPID  %get id of last process to operate on sem set0000251246450300  
%              SEM_GETVAL  %get the semaphore value                     0000251346450320  
%              SEM_GETALL  %get all semaphore values in the set         0000251446450340  
%              SEM_GETZCNT %get # procs waiting for sem value to be 0   0000251546450360  
%              SEM_SETVAL  %set a semaphore value                       0000251646450380  
%              SEM_SETALL  %set all semaphore value in the set          0000251746450400  
%          VAL (integer) - semaphore value for the SEM_SETVAL command   0000251846450420  
%          ARG (real array) - contains the following information:       0000251946450440  
%              If CMD = SEM_GETALL or SEM_SETALL, ARG contains every    0000252046450460  
%                 semvals in the set of semaphores.                     0000252146450480

%                 semid_ds structure. For more information about this   0000252346450520

%                 of this include file.                                 0000252546450560

DEFINE    SIGACTION(SIG, ACT_PROC, ACT, ACT_OFF, ACT_LEN,               0000282546660000

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000283146660120  
%   SIGACTION: allows the calling process to examine and/or specify     0000283246660140  
%              the action to be associated with a specific signal.      0000283346660160  
%                                                                       0000283446660180  
%   INPUT: SIG (integer) - signal number (refer to the sig values       0000283546660200  
%                          defined in this file)                        0000283646660220  
%          ACT_PROC - signal handling procedure to be invoked when a    0000283746660240  
%                     specified signal has been occurred. This procedure0000283846660260  
%                     must be declared as an integer procedure and take 0000283946660280  
%                     ten integer variables.                            0000284046660300  
%                                                                       0000284146660320  
%                INPUT:                                                 0000284246660340  
%                   INFO1 - version and signal number                   0000284346660360  
%                   INFO2 - sa_handler                                  0000284446660380  
%                   INFO3 - additional signal state information         0000284546660400  
%                   INFO4 - pid for si_pid member of siginfo structure  0000284646660420  
%                   INFO5 - uid for si_uid member of siginfo structure  0000284746660440  
%                   INFO6 - signal value for si_value member of the     0000284846660460  
%                           siginfo structure                           0000284946660480  
%                   INFO7 - for future expansion                        0000285046660500  
%                   INFO8 - for future expansion                        0000285146660520  
%                   INFO9 - for future expansion                        0000285246660540  
%                   INFO10 - for future expansion                       0000285346660560  
%                RETURN:                                                0000285446660580  
%                   result not used                                     0000285546660600  
%                                                                       0000285646660620  
%          ACT (real array) - the sigaction structure                   0000285746660640  
%              This structure contains the following 3 words:           0000285846660660  
%                sa_handler - SIG_FDFL, SIG_IGN or a unique number      0000285946660680  
%                             which is passed as the INFO2 parameter    0000286046660700  
%                             to the ACT_PROC procedure.                0000286146660720  
%                sa_mask    - a set of signals to be blocked during the 0000286246660740  
%                             execution of the signal-catching function 0000286346660760  
%                sa_flag    - flags which affect the behavior of signal 0000286446660780  
%                                                                       0000286546660800  
%          ACT_OFF (integer) - word offset into the array ACT           0000286646660820  
%                                                                       0000286746660840  
%          ACT_LEN (integer) -  the length of the sigaction structure   0000286846660860  
%              If the length is 0, then the ACT is a null structure.    0000286946660880  
%                                                                       0000287046660900  
%          OACT_OFF (integer) - word offset into the ACT structure      0000287146660920  
%          OACT_MAX (integer) - the length of the sigaction structure   0000287246660940  
%               If length is 0, then the ACT is a null structure.       0000287346660960  
%   OUTPUT:                                                             0000287446660980  
%          OACT (real array) - the sigaction structure                  0000287546661000  
%                                                                       0000287646661020  
%   RETURN: Upon successful completion, the procedure returns 0.        0000287746661040  
%           If an error occurs, the procedure returns -1 and ERRNO is   0000287846661060  
%           set to indicate the error.                                  0000287946661080

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000320146830060  
%   STAT: Returns the file status associated with the file named in     0000320246830070  
%          the PATH.                                                    0000320346830080  
%                                                                       0000320446830090  
%   INPUT:  For the description of PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE, 0000320546830100  
%           and PATH_SEARCHRULE, refer to the POSIX_ACCESS Pathname     0000320646830110  
%           defines in this file.                                       0000320746830120  
%           BUF_OFF (integer) - the word offset into the array BUF      0000320846830130  
%           BUF_MAX (integer) - max size in words starting from BUF_OFF 0000320946830140  
%                         to be filled in                               0000321046830150  
%                                                                       0000321146830160  
%   OUTPUT: BUF (real array) - holds the file status structure.         0000321246830170  
%           Refer to the Part 2 of this file for the definition of the  0000321346830180  
%           file status structure.                                      0000321446830190  
%                                                                       0000321546830200

%           file status structure in the array BUF.                     0000321746830220  
%           If BUF_MAX does not have enough space to hold the file      0000321846830230  
%           status structure, then it will be truncated.                0000321946830240  
%           If an error occurs, the procedure returns -1 and ERRNO is   0000322046830250  
%           set to indicate the error.                                  0000322146830260  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000322246830270  
                                                                        0000322346830280  
INTEGER PROCEDURE STRERROR(BUFF, BUFF_OFF, BUFF_MAX, ERRNO);            0000322446850080  
VALUE                            BUFF_OFF, BUFF_MAX, ERRNO;             0000322546850100  
REFERENCE                  BUFF;                                        0000322646850120  
EBCDIC ARRAY               BUFF[0];                                     0000322746850140  
INTEGER                          BUFF_OFF, BUFF_MAX, ERRNO;             0000322846850160  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000322946850180  
%   STRERROR: converts an error number to a string and returns the      0000323046850200  
%             string in the return argument.                            0000323146850220  
%                                                                       0000323246850240  
%   INPUT: ERRNO (integer) - error number                               0000323346850260  
%                                                                       0000323446850280  
%   OUTPUT: BUFF (EBCDIC array) - place holder for an error message     0000323546850300  
%           BUFF_OFF (integer) - byte offset into the array BUFF        0000323646850320  
%           BUFF_MAX (integer) - maximum length of the array BUFF       0000323746850340  
%                                                                       0000323846850360  
%   RETURN: length of string or -1 if an error occurred.                0000323946850380  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000324046850400  
BEGIN                                                                   0000324146850420  
   EBCDIC ARRAY EA[0:POSIX_ERR_ARRAY_SIZE-1];                           0000324246850440  
   INTEGER L;                                                           0000324346850460  
                                                                        0000324446850480  
   C_STRERROR(ERRNO, EA[0], FALSE);                                     0000324546850500  
   IF ERRNO >=  0 THEN                                                  0000324646850520  
   BEGIN                                                                0000324746850540  
      SCAN EA[0] FOR L:POSIX_ERR_ARRAY_SIZE UNTIL = 0;                  0000324846850560  
      L:=POSIX_ERR_ARRAY_SIZE-L;                                        0000324946850580  
      REPLACE BUFF[BUFF_OFF] BY EA[0] FOR MIN(BUFF_MAX,L);              0000325046850600  
      STRERROR := MIN(BUFF_MAX, L);                                     0000325146850610  
   END ELSE BEGIN                                                       0000325246850620  
      STRERROR := -1;                                                   0000325346850630  
   END;                                                                 0000325446850640  
END OF STRERROR;                                                        0000325546850650  
                                                                        0000325646850660  
DEFINE SVFORK(ERRNO) =                                                  0000325746850670  
       POSIX_FORK(SVFORKV, ERRNO) #;                                    0000325846850680  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000325946850690  
%   FORK:                                                               0000326046850700  
%        This procedure creates a clone of the entire calling process   0000326146850710  
%        by executing the top frame only.  Use it if you are going to   0000326246850720  
%        call exec() right away.  The parent thread is suspended        0000326346850730  
%        until the child() does an exec() or _exit().  After an exec(), 0000326446850740  
%        but before any user code is executed, the child waits on an MCP0000326546850750  
%        event.  The parent thread must call svfork_release(<child pid>)0000326646850760  
%        to allow the child to continue.  This gives the parent time    0000326746850770  
%        to enter information about the child before the child can      0000326846850780  
%        access it or exit().                                           0000326946850790  
%                                                                       0000327046850800  
%   INPUT: none                                                         0000327146850810  
%                                                                       0000327246850820  
%   OUTPUT: none                                                        0000327346850830  
%                                                                       0000327446850840  
%   RETURN: Upon successful completion, the procedure returns 0.        0000327546850850  
%           If an error occurs, the procedure returns -1 and ERRNO is   0000327646850860  
%           set to indicate the error.                                  0000327746850870  
%           If the function succeeds, a new process will be created that0000327846850880  
%           will be returned a zero;  the calling process will be       0000327946850890  
%           returned the MIX number of the new process.                 0000328046850900  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000328146850910  
                                                                        0000328246850920  
DEFINE SVFORK_RELEASE(PID, ERRNO) =                                     0000328346850925  
       POSIX_SETIDS(RELEASEVFORK_SV, PID, 0, ERRNO) #;                  0000328446850930  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000328546850935  
%   FORK:                                                               0000328646850940  
%        Releases the child thread created by svfork().                 0000328746850945  
%                                                                       0000328846850950  
%   INPUT: PID (integer) - the child's process id that is suspended     0000328946850955  
%                          after a svfork()/exec().                     0000329046850956  
%                                                                       0000329146850960

  GRABALOG(LOG,ALOG,NIVLOG,FECDIA,BLOG,DES,NIVDES)=                     0000371300276002  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000371400277002  
    IF NIVLOG = 0 THEN                                                  0000371500278002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000371600279002  
      REPLACE POINTER(BLOG) BY 48"00" FOR 990;                          0000371700279102  
      REPLACE POINTER(BLOG) BY "HD",                                    0000371800280002  
                               FECDIA  FOR 8 DIGITS,                    0000371900281002  
                               0       FOR 8 DIGITS,                    0000372000282002  
                               0       FOR 2 DIGITS;                    0000372100283002  
      WRITE(LOG[0],75,BLOG);                                            0000372200284002  
      LAST_RLOG[NUMDIAC1]:=LOG.LASTRECORD;                              0000372300285002  
    END;                                                                0000372400286002  
    REPLACE DFAUT(ALOG) BY (NIVLOG+1) FOR 8 DIGITS;                     0000372500286102  
    REPLACE DFAUTFAN(ALOG2) BY (NIVLOG+1) FOR 8 DIGITS;                 0000372600287102  
    IF IDFINDATADI(ALOG)  > 0 AND                                       0000372700301102  
       IDFFUNCION (ALOG)  = 1   THEN                                    0000372800301202  
    BEGIN                                                               0000372900302002  
       IF NIVDES = 0 THEN                                               0000373000303002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000373100304002  
         REPLACE POINTER(BLOG) BY "HD",FECDIA FOR 8 DIGITS;             0000373200305002  
         WRITE(DES[0],90,BLOG);                                         0000373300306002  
         LAST_RDES[NUMDIAC1]:=DES.LASTRECORD;                           0000373400307002  
         REPLACE POINTER(BLOG) BY "HD",                                 0000373500310002  
                                  FECDIA FOR   8 DIGITS,                0000373600311002  
                                  NIVLOG FOR   8 DIGITS,                0000373700312002  
                                  1      FOR   2 DIGITS,                0000373800313002  
                                  40"0"  FOR 338 ;                      0000373900314002  
         WRITE(LOG[0],75,BLOG);                                         0000374000315002  
       END;                                                             0000374100316002  
       APUN_DESC:= 0;                                                   0000374200317002  
       P_ALOG := POINTER(ALOG)+445;                                     0000374300345152  
       SCAN P_ALOG         FOR LEN_REG:530 UNTIL NEQ 48"00";            0000374400345202  
       IF  LEN_REG > 0 THEN                                             0000374500345252  
       BEGIN                                                            0000374600345302  
          REPLACE POINTER(BLOG,4) BY (NIVLOG +1) FOR 8 DIGITS,          0000374700345352  
                             POINTER(ALOG,4)+890 FOR 1060,              0000374800345402  
                                               0 FOR   2 DIGITS,        0000374900345452  
                             APUN_DESC           FOR   8 DIGITS,        0000375000345502  
                                             01  FOR   2 DIGITS;        0000375100345552  
          WRITE(DES[NIVDES+1],90,BLOG);                                 0000375200347002  
          NIVDES:= * + 1;                                               0000375300348002  
          APUN_DESC := NIVDES;                                          0000375400348012  
       END;                                                             0000375500349002  
                                                                        0000375600350002  
       REPLACE POINTER(BLOG,4) BY DFFUNCION(ALOG) FOR 890,              0000375700351002  
                               APUN_DESC          FOR 8  DIGITS,        0000375800352002  
                               1                  FOR 2  DIGITS %STATUS 0000375900353002  
                              ;                                         0000376000355002  
    END                                                                 0000376100356002  
    ELSE                                                                0000376200357002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000376300357102  
       REPLACE DFINDATADI(ALOG2) BY 0 FOR 2 DIGITS;                     0000376400357202  
       REPLACE POINTER(BLOG,4) BY DFFUNCION(ALOG) FOR 890,              0000376500358002  
                                                0 FOR 8 DIGITS,         0000376600359002  
                                                1 FOR 2 DIGITS %STATUS  0000376700361002  
                                 ;                                      0000376800362002  
    END;                                                                0000376900362102  
    WRITE(LOG[NIVLOG + 1],75,BLOG);                                     0000377000363002  
    NIVLOG:= * + 1;                                                     0000377100364002  
  END#,                                                                 0000377200365002  
%%         END OF GRABALOG                                              0000377300366002

  BORRAXPROC(LOGO,ALOG,FECDIA)=                                         0000390900493002  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000391000494002  
     NUM_REG:=0;                                                        0000391100495002  
     REG_LEIDOS:=-1;                                                    0000391200496002  
     CLOSE (LOGO,LOCK);                                                 0000391300497002  
     LOGO.BLOCKSIZE := 10800;                                           0000391400498002  
                                                                        0000391500499002  
    WHILE NOT READ(LOGO[REG_LEIDOS:= * + 1],75,ALOG) AND                0000391600500002  
            INTEGER(POINTER(ALOG,4),2) NEQ 99 DO                        0000391700501002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000391800502002  
       IF     IDFFUNCION(ALOG)  = 1                AND                  0000391900503002  
%             IDFSISTEMA(ALOG)  = IDFSISTEMA(ALOG) AND                  0000392000504002  
              IDFTIPPROC(ALOG)  = PROCAELIM        AND                  0000392100505002  
              IDFSTATUS (ALOG)  = 1               THEN                  0000392200506002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000392300507002  
            NUM_REG:= * + 1;                                            0000392400508002  
            REPLACE POINTER(ALOG,4)+ 899 BY 3 FOR 1 DIGITS;             0000392500509002  
            WRITE(LOGO[REG_LEIDOS],75,ALOG);                            0000392600510002  
       END;                                                             0000392700511002  
     END;                                                               0000392800512002  
     CLOSE (LOGO,LOCK);                                                 0000392900513002  
     LOGO.BLOCKSIZE := 150;                                             0000393000514002  
%    REPLACE   BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ);                                  0000393100515002  
%    REPLACE MSGLJ  BY "S151/L010/001:I>050110<";                       0000393200516002  
%    REPLACE TXTMSG BY "REG.ELIM.X PROC. ",                             0000393300517002  
%                      PROCAELIM  FOR * DIGITS, " CON FECHA ",          0000393400518002  
%                      FECDIA     FOR 8 DIGITS, " => ",                 0000393500519002  
%                      NUM_REG    FOR * DIGITS;                         0000393600520002  
%    LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                   0000393700521002  
  END#,                                                                 0000393800522002

  DFAMPLIAFILE(FILE_AMP,LEV_INI,ADD_REC,ARR_VACIO,REGSIZE)=             0000397900563002  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000398000564002  
     ERRORMSG;                                                          0000398100565002  
     REPLACE POINTER(ARR_VACIO,4) BY 99 FOR 2 DIGITS;                   0000398200570002  
     CLOSE(FILE_AMP,LOCK) ;                                             0000398300571002  
     BLOCK_SIZE := FILE_AMP.BLOCKSIZE;                                  0000398400572002  
     IF BLOCK_SIZE = 150 THEN                                           0000398500572102  
        FILE_AMP.SYNCHRONIZE := VALUE (NO);                             0000398600572202  
     FILE_AMP.BLOCKSIZE := 10800;                                       0000398700573002  
     FOR R:= LEV_INI STEP 1 UNTIL (LEV_INI + ADD_REC) DO                0000398800574002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000398900575002  
         WRITE (FILE_AMP[R],REGSIZE,ARR_VACIO);                         0000399000576002  
     END;                                                               0000399100577002  
     CLOSE(FILE_AMP,LOCK);                                              0000399200578002  
     IF BLOCK_SIZE = 150 THEN                                           0000399300578102  
        FILE_AMP.SYNCHRONIZE := VALUE (OUT);                            0000399400578202  
     FILE_AMP.BLOCKSIZE := BLOCK_SIZE;                                  0000399500579002  
  END#,                                                                 0000399600587002

              ITIPOMOVI := IDFESQOCUR(ALOG,GIND);                       0000458601121002

                     CARGA_CATCT[ICLAVTRAN, 1] = 0) THEN                0000458901124002

                  "ENVIO DE MOVIMIENTOS, TIPO DE MOVIMIENTO ERRONEO ",  0000460901144002

 INTEGER PROCEDURE CALINDSIS(SISTEMA,PRODUCTO);                         0000508201591002  
 VALUE   SISTEMA,PRODUCTO;                                              0000508301592002  
 INTEGER SISTEMA,PRODUCTO;                                              0000508401593002  
%***********************************                                    0000508501594002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000508601595002  
   CASE SISTEMA OF                                                      0000508701596002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000508801597002  
     500:CASE PRODUCTO OF                                               0000508901598002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000509001599002  
            066:CALINDSIS:=0;                                           0000509101600002  
            500:CALINDSIS:=1;                                           0000509201601002  
            001:CALINDSIS:=2;                                           0000509301602002  
           ELSE:CALINDSIS:=99;                                          0000509401603002  
         END;                                                           0000509501604002  
     084:CASE PRODUCTO OF                                               0000509601605002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000509701606002  
            084:CALINDSIS:=1;                                           0000509801607002  
           ELSE:CALINDSIS:=99;                                          0000509901608002  
         END;                                                           0000510001609002  
     087:CASE PRODUCTO OF                                               0000510101610002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000510201611002  
            087:CALINDSIS:=1;                                           0000510301612002  
           ELSE:CALINDSIS:=99;                                          0000510401613002  
         END;                                                           0000510501614002  
     408:CALINDSIS:=1;                                                  0000510601615002  
     151:IF PRODUCTO = 1151 THEN                                        0000510701616002  
            CALINDSIS:=1                                                0000510801617002  
         ELSE                                                           0000510901618002  
            CALINDSIS:=99;                                              0000511001619002  
     701:CALINDSIS:= 1;                                                 0000511101620002  
     703:CALINDSIS:= 1;                                                 0000511201621002  
     403:CALINDSIS:= 1;                                                 0000511301622002  
     404:CALINDSIS:= 1;                                                 0000511401623002  
     264:CALINDSIS:= 1;                                                 0000511501624002  
     333:CALINDSIS:= 1;                                                 0000511601625002  
     ELSE:CALINDSIS:=99;                                                0000511701626002  
   END;                                                                 0000511801627002  
 END OF CALINDSIS;                                                      0000511901628002

 BOOLEAN PROCEDURE EXTERNO_P015;                                        0000512701636002  
%*******************************************************************    0000512801637002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000512901638002  
    STAT_P015[NUMDIAC1].STATUS := VALUE(NEVERUSED);                     0000513001639002  
    REPLACE NOM_ASINC BY "(S151)S151/OBJECT/P015/ASINCS",               0000513101640002  
                           SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,                       0000513201641002  
                   FEC_DIA[NUMDIAC1] FOR 6 DIGITS,                      0000513301642002  
                           "/",                                         0000513401643002  
                           MTP_P015 FOR 8,                              0000513501644002  
                           " ON CMEMP.";                                0000513601645002  
                                                                        0000513701646002  
    REPLACE STAT_P015[NUMDIAC1].NAME BY NOM_ASINC;                      0000513801647002  
    REPLACE PARAM BY FEC_DIA[NUMDIAC1] FOR 8 DIGITS ,                   0000513901648002  
                NUMDIAC1+1             FOR 2 DIGITS ,                   0000514001649002  
                SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,");";                             0000514101650002  
    PROCESS EJECUTA(PARAM)[STAT_P015[NUMDIAC1]];                        0000514201651002  
    WAIT((3));                                                          0000514301652002  
    INTENTOS := 0;                                                      0000514401652102  
    WHILE STATUS_MIX_P015(NUMDIAC1) = 0 AND                             0000514501652202  
          INTENTOS < 30                 DO                              0000514601652302  
    BEGIN                                                               0000514701652402  
       WAIT((.3))        ;                                              0000514801652502  
       INTENTOS := * + 1 ;                                              0000514901652602  
    END;                                                                0000515001652702  
    REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                                  0000515101653002  
    REPLACE ARR1 BY "REMOVE ",                                          0000515201654002  
                    "(S151)S151/OBJECT/P015/ASINCS",                    0000515301655002  
                      SIST_LIB          FOR 3 DIGITS,                   0000515401656002  
                      FEC_DIA[NUMDIAC1] FOR 6 DIGITS,                   0000515501657002  
                      "/",                                              0000515601658002  
                      MTP_P015 FOR 8,                                   0000515701659002  
                      " ON CMEMP"                                       0000515801660002  
                      48"00";                                           0000515901661002  
    DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                        0000516001662002  
 END OF EXTERNO_P015;                                                   0000516101663002

%*******************************************************************    0000516401663032

    STAT_P016[NUMDIAC1].STATUS := VALUE(NEVERUSED);                     0000516601663052  
    REPLACE NOM_ASINC BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(NOM_ASINC);                    0000516701663062  
    REPLACE NOM_ASINC BY P016_X_SIS UNTIL = 48"00"  ,                   0000516801663072  
                         " ON CMEMP.";                                  0000516901663082  
    REPLACE PARAM BY 48"00" FOR 40;                                     0000517001663092  
    REPLACE STAT_P016[NUMDIAC1].NAME BY NOM_ASINC;                      0000517101663112  
    REPLACE PARAM BY FEC_DIA[NUMDIAC1] FOR 8 DIGITS ,                   0000517201663122  
                     SIST_LIB FOR 4 DIGITS,");"     ;                   0000517301663132  
    PROCESS EJECUTA(PARAM)[STAT_P016[NUMDIAC1]];                        0000517401663142  
    WAIT((3));                                                          0000517501663152  
    INTENTOS := 0;                                                      0000517601663154  
    WHILE STATUS_MIX_P016(NUMDIAC1) = 0 AND                             0000517701663156  
           INTENTOS < 30                 DO                             0000517801663158  
    BEGIN                                                               0000517901663160  
       WAIT((.3))        ;                                              0000518001663162  
       INTENTOS := * + 1 ;                                              0000518101663164  
    END;                                                                0000518201663166  
    REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                                  0000518301663168  
    REPLACE ARR1 BY "REMOVE " , NOM_ASINC UNTIL = ".",                  0000518401663172  
                    48"00";                                             0000518501663182  
    DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                        0000518601663192  
 END OF EXTERNO_P016;                                                   0000518701663202

%*******************************************************************    0000519101665002  
 BOOLEAN PROCEDURE EXTERNO_P025;                                        0000519201666002  
%*******************************************************************    0000519301667002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000519401668002  
    STAT_P025.STATUS := VALUE(NEVERUSED);                               0000519501669002  
    REPLACE CTLVER_ID BY "S151P025ACTS";                                0000519601670002  
    IF DAME_TIT(CTLVER_ID,CTLVER_NOM) < 0  THEN                         0000519701671002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000519801672002  
       REPLACE MSGLJ BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ);                            0000519901673002  
       REPLACE MSGLJ BY HDRMSG,"I>020901< LIBRARY REGISTRA, ",          0000520001674002  
               "ERROR EN CTLVERS, NO EXISTE IDENTIFICADOR DE ",         0000520101675002  
               "P025 ASINCRONO S151 (S151P025ACT)" ;                    0000520201676002  
       LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                 0000520301677002  
     REPLACE NOMP025 BY "(S151)S151/OBJECT/P025/ACTBDSDOMOV/02MTP   ";  0000520401678002  
    END                                                                 0000520501679002  
    ELSE                                                                0000520601680002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000520701681002  
       REPLACE NOMP025 BY CTLVER_NOM UNTIL = " ", " ";                  0000520801682002  
       POSICION := SIZE(NOMP025);                                       0000520901683002  
       IF POSICION > 16 THEN                                            0000521001684002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000521101685002  
          POINT_P025 := NOMP025[POSICION - 16];                         0000521201686002  
          REPLACE MTP_P025 BY POINT_P025 FOR 8;                         0000521301687002  
       END                                                              0000521401688002  
       ELSE                                                             0000521501689002  
          REPLACE MTP_P025 BY "02MTP   ";                               0000521601690002  
    END;                                                                0000521701691002  
    REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                                  0000521801692002  
    REPLACE ARR1 BY "COPY ", NOMP025 UNTIL = " " ,                      0000521901693002  
                    " AS ",                                             0000522001694002  
                    "(S151)S151/OBJECT/P025/ACTBDS",                    0000522101695002  
                      SIST_LIB          FOR 3 DIGITS,                   0000522201696002  
                      "/",                                              0000522301697002  
                      MTP_P025 FOR 8,                                   0000522401698002  
                      " FROM CMEMP(PACK) TO CMEMP(PACK)",               0000522501699002  
                      48"00";                                           0000522601700002  
    DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                        0000522701701002  
    REPLACE NOM_ASINC BY "(S151)S151/OBJECT/P025/ACTBDS",               0000522801702002  
                          SIST_LIB          FOR 3 DIGITS,               0000522901703002  
                          "/",                                          0000523001704002  
                          MTP_P025 FOR 8,                               0000523101705002  
                          " ON CMEMP.",                                 0000523201706002  
                          48"00";                                       0000523301707002  
    REPLACE STAT_P025.NAME BY NOM_ASINC;                                0000523401708002  
    REPLACE PARAM BY SIST_LIB          FOR 3 DIGITS ;                   0000523501709002  
    PROCESS EJECUTA(PARAM)[STAT_P025];                                  0000523601710002  
    WAIT((3));                                                          0000523701711002  
 END OF EXTERNO_P025;                                                   0000523801712002

 PROCEDURE CARGAMEMORY;                                                 0000539701740002  
%----------------------                                                 0000539801741002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000539901742002  
   IF IDFFUNCION(ALOG1) = 1 THEN                                        0000540001743002  
      DFVALIDA(ALOG1,IDFSISTEMA(ALOG1),IDFPRODUCT(ALOG1),GIND1);        0000540101744002  
   IF GRABAMOV  THEN                                                    0000540201745002  
   GRABALOG(LOGS[NUMDIAC1],ALOG1,NIVLOG_A[NUMDIAC1],FEC_DIA[NUMDIAC1],  0000540301746002  
                              BLOG1,DESS[NUMDIAC1],NIVDES_A[NUMDIAC1]); 0000540401747002  
                                                                        0000540501748002  
   IF LAST_RLOG[NUMDIAC1] - NIVLOG_A[NUMDIAC1] < 5 THEN                 0000540601749002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000540701750002  
      REG_SIZE := 75;                                                   0000540801750102  
      AMPL_REG := 20000;                                                0000540901750202  
      DFAMPLIAFILE(LOGS[NUMDIAC1],LAST_RLOG[NUMDIAC1],AMPL_REG,A_VACIO, 0000541001751002  
                   REG_SIZE);                                           0000541101751102  
      READ(LOGS[NUMDIAC1][0]);                                          0000541201752002  
      LAST_RLOG[NUMDIAC1]:=LOGS[NUMDIAC1].LASTRECORD;                   0000541301753002  
   END;                                                                 0000541401754002  
                                                                        0000541501755002  
                                                                        0000541601756002  
   IF LAST_RDES[NUMDIAC1] - NIVDES_A[NUMDIAC1] < 5 THEN                 0000541701757002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000541801758002  
      REG_SIZE := 90;                                                   0000541901758102  
      AMPL_REG := 5000;                                                 0000542001758202  
      DFAMPLIAFILE(DESS[NUMDIAC1],LAST_RDES[NUMDIAC1],AMPL_REG,A_VACIO, 0000542101759002  
                   REG_SIZE);                                           0000542201759102  
      READ(DESS[NUMDIAC1][0]);                                          0000542301760002  
      LAST_RDES[NUMDIAC1]:=DESS[NUMDIAC1].LASTRECORD;                   0000542401761002  
   END;                                                                 0000542501762002  
                                                                        0000542601763002  
 END OF CARGAMEMORY;                                                    0000542701764002

 PROCEDURE REBLOCKADE;                                                  0000550101838002  
%---------------------                                                  0000550201839002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000550301840002  
   INTEGER BLOQUE;                                                      0000550401841002  
                                                                        0000550501842002  
   CARGAMEMORY ;                                                        0000550601843002  
   IF IDFFUNCION(ALOG1) = 31 THEN                                       0000550701844002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000550801845002  
      CLOSE (LOGS[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                      0000550901846002  
      CLOSE (DESS[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                      0000551001847002  
     LOGS[NUMDIAC1].BLOCKSIZE := 10800;                                 0000551101848002  
     LOGS[NUMDIAC1].SYNCHRONIZE := VALUE (NO);                          0000551201848102  
     DESS[NUMDIAC1].BLOCKSIZE := 10800;                                 0000551301849002  
      IF LOGS[NUMDIAC1].RESIDENT THEN                                   0000551401850002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000551501851002  
         READ(LOGS[NUMDIAC1][0]);                                       0000551601852002  
         LAST_RLOG[NUMDIAC1]:=LOGS[NUMDIAC1].LASTRECORD;                0000551701853002  
      END;                                                              0000551801854002  
      IF DESS[NUMDIAC1].RESIDENT THEN                                   0000551901855002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000552001856002  
         READ(DESS[NUMDIAC1][0]);                                       0000552101857002  
         LAST_RDES[NUMDIAC1]:=DESS[NUMDIAC1].LASTRECORD;                0000552201858002  
      END;                                                              0000552301859002  
   END                                                                  0000552401860002  
   ELSE                                                                 0000552501861002  
   IF IDFFUNCION(ALOG1) = 32 THEN                                       0000552601862002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000552701863002  
      CLOSE (LOGS[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                      0000552801864002  
      CLOSE (DESS[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                      0000552901865002  
      LOGS[NUMDIAC1].BLOCKSIZE := 150;                                  0000553001866002  
      LOGS[NUMDIAC1].SYNCHRONIZE := VALUE (OUT);                        0000553101866102  
      DESS[NUMDIAC1].BLOCKSIZE := 90;                                   0000553201867002  
      IF LOGS[NUMDIAC1].RESIDENT THEN                                   0000553301868002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000553401869002  
         READ(LOGS[NUMDIAC1][0]);                                       0000553501870002  
         LAST_RLOG[NUMDIAC1]:=LOGS[NUMDIAC1].LASTRECORD;                0000553601871002  
      END;                                                              0000553701872002  
      IF DESS[NUMDIAC1].RESIDENT THEN                                   0000553801873002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000553901874002  
         READ(DESS[NUMDIAC1][0]);                                       0000554001875002  
         LAST_RDES[NUMDIAC1]:=DESS[NUMDIAC1].LASTRECORD;                0000554101876002  
      END;                                                              0000554201877002  
   END;                                                                 0000554301878002  
 % ACTNIVEL;                                                            0000554401879002  
 END;                                                                   0000554501880002

 PROCEDURE PREFINAL;                                                    0000554801883002  
%---------------------                                                  0000554901884002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000555001885002  
   CARGAMEMORY ;                                                        0000555101886002  
   CLOSE (LOGS[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                         0000555201887002  
   CLOSE (DESS[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                         0000555301888002  
   IF LOGS[NUMDIAC1].RESIDENT THEN                                      0000555401889002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000555501890002  
      READ(LOGS[NUMDIAC1][0]);                                          0000555601891002  
      LAST_RLOG[NUMDIAC1]:=LOGS[NUMDIAC1].LASTRECORD;                   0000555701892002  
   END;                                                                 0000555801893002  
   IF DESS[NUMDIAC1].RESIDENT THEN                                      0000555901894002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000556001895002  
      READ(DESS[NUMDIAC1][0]);                                          0000556101896002  
      LAST_RDES[NUMDIAC1]:=DESS[NUMDIAC1].LASTRECORD;                   0000556201897002  
   END;                                                                 0000556301898002  
%  ACTNIVEL;                                                            0000556401899002  
 END;                                                                   0000556501900002
