%---------------------                                                  0000556801903002  
 PROCEDURE FINAL;                                                       0000556901904002  
%---------------------                                                  0000557001905002  
                                                                        0000557101906002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000557201907002  
   INTEGER  REINTENTOS;                                                 0000557301908002  
   CARGAMEMORY ;                                                        0000557401909002  
   CLOSE (LOGS[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                         0000557501910002  
   CLOSE (DESS[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                         0000557601911002  
   CLOSE (SDOS[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                         0000557701912002  
%  ACTNIVEL;                                                            0000557801913002  
   REINTENTOS := 0;                                                     0000557901914002  
   RESULT_CTL := 1;                                                     0000558001915002  
   WHILE RESULT_CTL > 0 AND REINTENTOS < 3 DO                           0000558101916002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000558201917002  
      REPLACE ACONTROLE1 BY 0 FOR SIZE(ACONTROLE1);                     0000558301918002  
      REPLACE FUNB01F05(ACONTROLE1) BY     5 FOR 2 DIGITS,              0000558401919002  
                                    SIST_LIB FOR 4 DIGITS,              0000558501920002  
                                    CSI_LIB  FOR 2 DIGITS,              0000558601921002  
                                           0 FOR 2 DIGITS;              0000558701922002  
      RESULT_CTL := CONSISDIA(ACONTROLE1);                              0000558801923002  
      REINTENTOS := * + 1;                                              0000558901924002  
   END;                                                                 0000559001925002  
   IF RESULT_CTL > 0 THEN                                               0000559101926002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000559201927002  
     REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR EN FUNCION 98 ",                          0000559301928002  
                       "L002/REGISTRAS",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,          0000559401929002  
                      ", RESULT L001/CONTROL ", RESULT_CTL FOR 6 DIGITS,0000559501930002  
                      " NO SE MODIF. ESTATUS DE PROY. FECHA ",          0000559601931002  
                      " PARA ", ISISB01F05(ACONTROLE1) FOR 3 DIGITS;    0000559701932002  
     LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                   0000559801933002  
   END;                                                                 0000559901934002  
                                                                        0000560001935002  
 END;                                                                   0000560101936002  
%                                                                       0000560201937002  
%------------------------                                               0000560301938002  
% OUTER BLOCK OF CARGAMOV                                               0000560401939002  
%------------------------                                               0000560501940002  
 RESULT:=0;                                                             0000560601941002  
 GRABAMOV:= TRUE;                                                       0000560701941102  
 SIZEDATC1:=SIZE(DATOS);                                                0000560801943002  
 IF SIZEDATC1 > 540 THEN                                                0000560901944002  
    SIZEDATC1:= 540;                                                    0000561001945002  
%REPLACE EA_I_CPST[0] BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(EA_I_CPST);                    0000561101946002  
 REPLACE POINTER(ALOG1) BY 48"00"  FOR 540;                             0000561201947002  
 REPLACE POINTER(BLOG1) BY 48"00"  FOR 540;                             0000561301948002  
 REPLACE POINTER(ALOG2) BY 48"00"  FOR 540;                             0000561401948102  
 REPLACE POINTER(ALOG1) BY DATOS FOR SIZEDATC1;                         0000561501949002  
 REPLACE POINTER(ALOG2) BY DATOS FOR SIZEDATC1;                         0000561601949102  
 OPC_MSG := 1;                                                          0000561701949202  
 LNG_MSG := 1901;                                                       0000561801949302  
%ARMA_MSG_MON(HALOG_CBII);                                              0000561901949402  
%MONITOREA(OPC_MSG,MSG_MON,LNG_MSG);                                    0000562001949502  
 IF SIST_LIB = 500 OR SIST_LIB = 403 OR SIST_LIB = 404                  0000562101950002  
 THEN                                                                   0000562201951002  
 ELSE                                                                   0000562301952002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000562401953002  
    REPLACE POINTER(ALOG1,4)+342 BY 2 FOR 3 DIGITS;                     0000562501954002  
    REPLACE POINTER(ALOG1,4)+345 BY 2 FOR 3 DIGITS;                     0000562601955002  
 END;                                                                   0000562701956002  
 WHILE LOCKSTATUS(CAMBIA_FECHA).[1:2] > 0 DO WAIT((.3))  ;              0000562801957002  
 IF IDFSISTFAN(ALOG1) NEQ SIST_LIB THEN                                 0000562901958002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000563001959002  
       REPLACE EA_I_CPST BY                                             0000563101960002  
               "SISTEMA NO HABILITADO EN S151, ",                       0000563201961002  
               "SISTEMA : ", IDFSISTEMA(ALOG1) FOR 4 DIGITS ;           0000563301962002  
       I_TIPO := 3;                                                     0000563401963002  
%      L002COMMPOST(EA_I_CPST,I_TIPO);                                  0000563501964002  
       RESULT := 1;                                                     0000563601965002  
       GRABAMOV := FALSE;                                               0000563701965102  
    END;                                                                0000563801967002  
                                                                        0000563901967102  
  IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                                    0000564001967202  
     MAPEO;                                                             0000564101967302  
                                                                        0000564201968002  
 I_I    := -1;                                                          0000564301969002  
 BOOL_I := FALSE ;                                                      0000564401970002  
 IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                                     0000564501971002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000564601972002  
    IF NOM_EQUIPO = "ACYPBETA" OR NOM_EQUIPO = "VDMBETA" THEN           0000564701973002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000564801974002  
        WHILE I_I:= * + 1 LEQ 9 AND NOT BOOL_I DO                       0000564901975002  
        BEGIN                                                           0000565001976002  
           IF IDFFECCONT(ALOG1) = FEC_DIA[I_I] THEN                     0000565101977002  
           BEGIN                                                        0000565201978002  
              NUMDIAC1 := I_I ;                                         0000565301979002  
              BOOL_I   := TRUE;                                         0000565401980002  
           END;                                                         0000565501981002  
        END;                                                            0000565601982002  
        IF NOT BOOL_I THEN                                              0000565701983002  
           BEGIN                                                        0000565801984002  
              RESULT := 4;                                              0000565901985002  
              NUMDIAC1 := DIA_SEMANA;                                   0000566001986002  
           END                                                          0000566101987002  
       END                                                              0000566201988002  
    ELSE                                                                0000566301989002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000566401990002  
       IF IDFFECCONT(ALOG1) = FECHAPROC THEN                            0000566501991002  
          NUMDIAC1 := DIA_SEMANA                                        0000566601992002  
       ELSE                                                             0000566701993002  
       IF IDFFECCONT(ALOG1) > FECHAPROC THEN                            0000566801994002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000566901995002  
          I_I    := -1;                                                 0000567001996002  
          BOOL_I := FALSE ;                                             0000567101997002  
          WHILE I_I:= * + 1 LEQ 9 AND NOT BOOL_I DO                     0000567201998002  
          BEGIN                                                         0000567301999002  
             IF IDFFECCONT(ALOG1) = FEC_DIA[I_I] THEN                   0000567402000002  
             BEGIN                                                      0000567502001002  
                NUMDIAC1 := I_I ;                                       0000567602002002  
                BOOL_I   := TRUE;                                       0000567702003002  
             END;                                                       0000567802004002  
          END;                                                          0000567902005002  
          IF NOT BOOL_I THEN                                            0000568002006002  
          BEGIN                                                         0000568102007002  
             RESULT := 4;                                               0000568202008002  
             NUMDIAC1 := DIA_SEMANA;                                    0000568302009002  
          END;                                                          0000568402010002  
       END                                                              0000568502011002  
       ELSE                                                             0000568602012002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000568702013002  
          NUMDIAC1 := DIA_SEMANA;                                       0000568802014002  
          RESULT   := 4;                                                0000568902015002  
       END;                                                             0000569002016002  
    END;                                                                0000569102017002  
 END;                                                                   0000569202018002  
 IF RESULT = 4 THEN                                                     0000569302019002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000569402020002  
    REPLACE EA_I_CPST BY                                                0000569502021002  
            "FECHA CONTABLE NO VALIDA, ",                               0000569602022002  
            "FECHA : ", IDFFECCONT(ALOG1) FOR 8 DIGITS ;                0000569702023002  
    I_TIPO := 3;                                                        0000569802024002  
 END;                                                                   0000569902025002  
 IF RESULT = 0 OR RESULT = 4 THEN                                       0000570002026002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000570102027002  
    FUNCIONVAL:= IDFFUNCION(ALOG1);                                     0000570202028002  
    CASE FUNCIONVAL OF                                                  0000570302029002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000570402030002  
       1:CARGAMEMORY;                                                   0000570502031002  
       2:ELIMINA;                                                       0000570602032002  
      11:INICIA;                                                        0000570702033002  
      12:TERMINA;                                                       0000570802034002  
      21:ELIMXPROC;                                                     0000570902035002  
      22:ELIMXAUT;                                                      0000571002036002  
      31:REBLOCKADE;                                                    0000571102037002  
      32:REBLOCKADE;                                                    0000571202038002  
      97:PREFINAL;                                                      0000571302039002  
      98:FINAL;                                                         0000571402040002  
    ELSE:                                                               0000571502041002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000571602042002  
            REPLACE EA_I_CPST BY                                        0000571702043002  
                    "FUNCION NO VALIDA, ",                              0000571802044002  
                    "FUNCION : ", IDFFUNCION(ALOG1) FOR 2 DIGITS ;      0000571902045002  
            I_TIPO := 3;                                                0000572002046002  
%           L002COMMPOST(EA_I_CPST,I_TIPO);                             0000572102047002  
            RESULT:= 2;                                                 0000572202048002  
            GRABAMOV := FALSE;                                          0000572302049002  
         END;                                                           0000572402050002  
    END;                                                                0000572502051002  
    IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                                  0000572602052002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000572702053002  
       REPLACE HHMMSS BY TIME(9) FOR 6   ;                              0000572802053004  
       HORA := INTEGER(POINTER(HHMMSS),6);                              0000572902053006  
       IF FUNCIONVAL < 98                       AND                     0000573002053008  
          (STATUS_MIX_P015(NUMDIAC1) < 1  OR                            0000573102053012  
          (STATUS_MIX_P016(NUMDIAC1) < 1 AND HORA < 200000))  AND       0000573202053022  
          (FEC_DIA[NUMDIAC1] = FEC_CON    OR                            0000573302054002  
           FEC_DIA[NUMDIAC1] > FEC_CON   )      AND                     0000573402055002  
           IDFTIPPROC(ALOG1) < 16               AND                     0000573502055012  
           LEVANTA_P015                         THEN                    0000573602056002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000573702057002  
          CLOSE (LOGS[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                  0000573802090102  
          CLOSE (DESS[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                  0000573902090202  
          LOGS[NUMDIAC1].BLOCKSIZE := 150;                              0000574002090302  
          LOGS[NUMDIAC1].SYNCHRONIZE := VALUE (OUT);                    0000574102090402  
          DESS[NUMDIAC1].BLOCKSIZE := 90;                               0000574202090502  
          IF STATUS_MIX_P015(NUMDIAC1)  < 1 AND                         0000574302093002  
             (STATUS_MIX_P016(NUMDIAC1) < 1 AND HORA < 200000)  THEN    0000574402093102  
          BEGIN                                                         0000574502093202  
             VERSION_P015;                                              0000574602093302  
             VERSION_P016;                                              0000574702093402  
             WAIT((3));                                                 0000574802094002  
             EXTERNO_P015;                                              0000574902094102  
             EXTERNO_P016;                                              0000575002094202  
          END                                                           0000575102094302  
          ELSE                                                          0000575202094402  
             IF STATUS_MIX_P015(NUMDIAC1) < 1 THEN                      0000575302094502  
             BEGIN                                                      0000575402094602  
                VERSION_P015;                                           0000575502094702  
                WAIT((3));                                              0000575602094802  
                EXTERNO_P015;                                           0000575702095002  
             END                                                        0000575802095102  
             ELSE                                                       0000575902095202  
                IF STATUS_MIX_P016(NUMDIAC1) < 1 AND                    0000576002095302  
                   HORA < 200000                 THEN                   0000576102095402  
                BEGIN                                                   0000576202095502  
                   VERSION_P016;                                        0000576302095602  
                   WAIT((3));                                           0000576402095702  
                   EXTERNO_P016;                                        0000576502095802  
                END;                                                    0000576602095902  
       END;                                                             0000576702096002  
       IF STATUS_MIX_P025 < 1 AND FEC_DIA[NUMDIAC1] = FECS151           0000576802097002  
          AND  FUNCIONVAL = 1 AND IDFTIPPROC(ALOG1) > 15 AND            0000576902098002  
          STATUS_BDSDO < 99 THEN                                        0000577002099002  
          EXTERNO_P025;                                                 0000577102100002  
                                                                        0000577202101002  
%      CAUSE (HAYMOV[NUMDIAC1]);                                        0000577302102002  
    END;                                                                0000577402103002  
 END;                                                                   0000577502104002  
 IF RESULT = 0 OR RESULT = 4 THEN                                       0000577602105002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000577702106002  
    ACT_NIVEL := * + 1;                                                 0000577802107002  
    IF IDFFUNCION(ALOG1) = 1 OR IDFFUNCION(ALOG1) = 2                   0000577902108002  
    THEN                                                                0000578002109002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000578102110002  
       IF ACT_NIVEL = 1000                                              0000578202111002  
       THEN                                                             0000578302112002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000578402113002  
          ACT_NIVEL := 0;                                               0000578502114002  
          ACTNIVEL;                                                     0000578602115002  
       END;                                                             0000578702116002  
    END                                                                 0000578802117002  
    ELSE                                                                0000578902118002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000579002119002  
       ACTNIVEL;                                                        0000579102120002  
       ACT_NIVEL := 0;                                                  0000579202121002  
    END;                                                                0000579302122002  
 END;                                                                   0000579402123002  
 IF RESULT > 0 THEN                                                     0000579502124002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000579602125002  
%      REPLACE MSGLJ BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ) ;                           0000579702126002  
%      REPLACE MSGLJ BY HDRMSG,"E>050110<",                             0000579802127002  
%                       "MENSAJE NO RECONOCIDO DEL S",                  0000579902128002  
%                       SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,                          0000580002129002

%                       RESULT FOR * DIGITS;                            0000580202131002  
%      LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                 0000580302132002

       REPLACE POINTER(ALOG2,4)+1078 BY RESULT FOR 2 DIGITS;            0000580502135102  
       WRITE(ERRORES1[NIVERROR1],90,ALOG2);                             0000580602135202  
       NIVERROR1 := * + 1;                                              0000580702136002  
    END;                                                                0000580802137002  
 REPLACE DATOS BY POINTER(ALOG2) FOR SIZEDATC1;                         0000580902138102  
 CARGAMOV1:= RESULT;                                                    0000581002139002  
                                                                        0000581102140002  
  IF RESULT > 0 THEN                                                    0000581202141002  
     IF NUM_ERROR[RESULT] > 0 THEN                                      0000581302142002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000581402143002  
        REPLACE EA_I_CPST[0] BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(EA_I_CPST);             0000581502144002  
        NUM_ERROR[RESULT] := * + 1;                                     0000581602145002  
        IF (NUM_ERROR[RESULT] - ((NUM_ERROR[RESULT] DIV 100 )           0000581702146002  
                               * 100)) = 0 THEN                         0000581802147002  
           BEGIN                                                        0000581902148002  
              REPLACE EA_I_CPST BY                                      0000582002149002  
                   "LLEVO ", NUM_ERROR[RESULT] FOR 8 DIGITS,            0000582102150002  
                   ", MENSAJES DE ERROR DEL TIPO DE RESULT = ",         0000582202151002  
                      RESULT FOR 2 DIGITS;                              0000582302152002  
              I_TIPO := 2;                                              0000582402153002  
              L002COMMPOST(EA_I_CPST,I_TIPO);                           0000582502154002

     END                                                                0000582702156002

               "FECHA CONTABLE NO VALIDA EN SALDOS";                    0000591402244002

%      L002COMMPOST(EA_I_CPST,I_TIPO);                                  0000591602246002

    END                                                                 0000591802248002

    CASE IDFSDOFUNC(ASDO1) OF                                           0000592102251002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000592202252002  
       80:BEGIN                                                         0000592302253002  
             NIVSDO_A[NUM_FEC] := 0   ;                                 0000592402254002

          END;                                                          0000593302261002  
       81:CARGAMEMSDO;                                                  0000593402262002  
       82:BEGIN                                                         0000593502263002  
             CARGAMEMSDO;                                               0000593602264002  
             CLOSE (SDOS[NUM_FEC],LOCK);                                0000593702265002  
          END;                                                          0000593802266002  
       ELSE:                                                            0000593902267002

%              L002COMMPOST(EA_I_CPST,I_TIPO);                          0000594402272002  
               RESULT_SDO:= 2;                                          0000594502273002  
            END;                                                        0000594602274002  
    END;                                                                0000594702275002  
 END;                                                                   0000594802276002

    BEGIN                                                               0000595002278002

       WRITE(ERRORES1[NIVERROR1],90,ASDO1);                             0000595202287002  
       NIVERROR1 := * + 1;                                              0000595302288002  
    END;                                                                0000595402289002

%*************************************************************          0000598902324002  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE CONTROLES(DATOS);                                    0000599002325002  
 EBCDIC ARRAY DATOS[*];                                                 0000599102326002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000599202327002  
%*************************************************************          0000599302328002

                                                                        0000599502330002  
        FUNCION       = INTEGER(DATOS[0],4)   #,                        0000599602331002  
        NUMLOG        = INTEGER(DATOS[4],2)   #;                        0000599702332002

%----------------------                                                 0000600002335002  
% CONTROLES OUTER BLOCK                                                 0000600102336002  
%----------------------                                                 0000600202337002  
% ACQUIREL(E3,A3);                                                      0000600302338002

  BEGIN                                                                 0000600602341002  
     1:                                                                 0000600702342002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000600802343002

         WHILE K:= * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                       0000601602351002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000601702352002  
            REPLACE TXTMSG BY "** FECHA ", K+1  FOR * DIGITS, " ",      0000601802353002  
                                  FEC_DIA[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS,             0000601902354002  
                    ", NIVEL = ", NIVLOG_A[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS, " **";     0000602002355002  
            LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                            0000602102356002  
         END;                                                           0000602202357002  
         RESULT:=0                                                      0000602302358002  
       END;                                                             0000602402359002  
                                                                        0000602502360002  
     2:                                                                 0000602602361002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000602702362002

         REPLACE MSGLJ BY "S151/L002/023:I>050110<";                    0000602902364002

            LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                            0000603902374002

         RESULT:=0                                                      0000604102376002  
       END;                                                             0000604202377002  
     3:                                                                 0000604302378002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000604402379002

         REPLACE MSGLJ BY "S151/L002/023:I>050110<";                    0000604602381002  
         REPLACE TXTMSG BY "** NIVELES DE ACT. EN S151 ",               0000604702382002

                           FEC_CON FOR 8 DIGITS;                        0000604902384002

         WHILE K:= * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                       0000605202387002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000605302388002  
            REPLACE TXTMSG BY " FECHA ", K+1  FOR * DIGITS, " ",        0000605402389002  
                                  FEC_DIA[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS,             0000605502390002  
                    ", NIVEL = ", NIVLOG_A[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS;            0000605602391002  
            LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                            0000605702392002  
            IF FEC_DIA[K] = FEC_CON THEN                                0000605802393002  
               IF STATUS_MIX_P015(K) > 0 THEN                           0000605902394002  
               BEGIN                                                    0000606002395002  
                  REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                    0000606102396002

                                  " HI 20 ",                            0000606302398002  
                                    48"00";                             0000606402399002  
                  DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                          0000606502400002  
               END;                                                     0000606602401002

 BEGIN                                                                  0000644602691002

                      ", CATALOGO S080 VACIO, ",                        0000644802693002

 ELSE                                                                   0000645302698002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000645402699002

                                   SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,               0000646502710002  
                                   "            ";                      0000646602711002

 ELSE                                                                   0000646802713002  
 IF FORMATO = 3 THEN                                                    0000646902714002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000647002715002  
    REPLACE PTR_HEX:PTR_HEX     BY 200101 FOR 6 DIGITS;                 0000647102716002  
    REPLACE PTR_EBC:PTR_EBC     BY "            ",                      0000647202717002  
                                   "            ";                      0000647302718002  
 END                                                                    0000647402719002  
 ELSE                                                                   0000647502720002

 BEGIN                                                                  0000647702722002  
    REPLACE PTR_HEX:PTR_HEX     BY 200201 FOR 6 DIGITS;                 0000647802723002

                                   "        ",                          0000648002725002

                                0000000 FOR 6 DIGITS,                   0000649702742002

%                               000000000 FOR 8 DIGITS,                 0000650002745002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000650102746002  
                                00      FOR 2 DIGITS;                   0000650202747002  
 SIST := 151;                                                           0000650302748002  
 RESULTADO := 0;                                                        0000650402749002  
 PROG := 002;                                                           0000650502750002  
 BOOL_CAT := FALSE ;                                                    0000650602751002  
 REPLACE PAR_SAL[0] BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(PAR_SAL);                        0000650702752002  
 WHILE RESULTADO < 2 DO                                                 0000650802753002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000650902754002  
    RESULTADO := L710_CONSUL_DETALLE(SIST,PROG,PAR_ENT,PAR_SAL);        0000651002755002

            "@" FOR 4;                                                  0000651302758002

    FECINI:= IDFSALFECONS(REG);                                         0000651602761002  
    NIVINI:= IDFSALNIVINI(REG);                                         0000651702762002  
    REPLACE PTR_HEX:PTR_HEX BY FECINI FOR 6 DIGITS,NIVINI FOR 2 DIGITS; 0000651802763002  
    IF FORMATO = 2 THEN                                                 0000651902764002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000652002765002  
      CLAVETR:=IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,2);                                     0000652102766002  
      NAT    :=IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,11);                                    0000652202767002  
      INDS250:=IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,12);                                    0000652302768002  
    END                                                                 0000652402769002  
    ELSE                                                                0000652502770002  
    IF FORMATO = 3 THEN                                                 0000652602771002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000652702772002  
      NAT       :=IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,6);                                  0000652802773002

    END                                                                 0000653702782002  
    ELSE                                                                0000653802783002

                               DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,2) FOR 12,               0000654602791002  
                               DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,3) FOR 12,               0000654702792002  
                               DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,4) FOR 12,               0000654802793002  
                               DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,5) FOR 12,               0000654902794002

    IF RESULTADO > 1 AND NOT BOOL_CAT THEN                              0000655102796002

    IF RESULTADO = 0 THEN RESULTADO:=3;                                 0000655902804002  
    BOOL_CAT:=TRUE;                                                     0000656002805002  
    IF NAT = 1 OR NAT = 2 THEN                                          0000656102806002  
       IF INDS250 = 2 THEN                                              0000656202807002  
          REPLACE ADFCVEINDIC(CLAVETR) BY 3   FOR 1 DIGITS              0000656302808002  
       ELSE                                                             0000656402809002  
          REPLACE ADFCVEINDIC(CLAVETR) BY 1   FOR 1 DIGITS              0000656502810002

                                                                        0000657102816002  
    CARGA_CATCT[ICVETRAN,(ITIPMOV-1)] := 1;                             0000657202817002

   ARRAY   ALOG[0:59];                                                  0000658402829002  
   INTEGER I,J,RESULT,RESULT_CTL,K,INT;                                 0000658502830002  
   INTEGER ARRAY NIVEL_K[0:9];                                          0000658602831002

%----------------------------------------------                         0000658902834002  
 PROCEDURE ARMALOG(LOGN,NIVLOG,FECDIA,NIVBASE);                         0000659002835002  
%----------------------------------------------                         0000659102836002  
 VALUE LOGN,FECDIA,NIVBASE;                                             0000659202837002  
 INTEGER LOGN,NIVLOG,FECDIA,NIVBASE;                                    0000659302838002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000659402839002  
   LABEL VERIFICA;                                                      0000659502840002  
                                                                        0000659602841002  
   INTEGER I;                                                           0000659702842002  
                                                                        0000659802843002  
   NIVLOG:= 0;                                                          0000659902844002  
   VERIFICA:                                                            0000660002845002  
                                                                        0000660102846002  
                                                                        0000660202847002  
   REPLACE NOM_ARCMOV BY ARC_MOV FOR 34,                                0000660302848002  
                         FECDIA  FOR 6 DIGITS,                          0000660402849002  
                         " ON "              ,                          0000660502850002  
                         PK_MOV  FOR 17,"."  ;                          0000660602851002  
                                                                        0000660702852002  
   REPLACE LOGS[LOGN].TITLE BY NOM_ARCMOV;                              0000660802853002  
   CLOSE(LOGS[LOGN],LOCK);                                              0000660902854002  
   LOGS[LOGN].MYUSE:= 3;                                                0000661002855002  
                                                                        0000661102856002  
   IF LOGS[LOGN].RESIDENT THEN                                          0000661202857002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000661302858002  
    READ(LOGS[LOGN][0],75,ALOG);                                        0000661402859002  
     LAST_RLOG[LOGN]:=LOGS[LOGN].LASTRECORD;                            0000661502860002  
     IF HFUNCION NEQ 99 THEN                                            0000661602861002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000661702862002  
        WHILE HFECLOG NEQ FECDIA DO                                     0000661802863002  
        BEGIN                                                           0000661902864002  
          CLOSE(LOGS[LOGN],LOCK);                                       0000662002865002  
          REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR LIB/REGISTRAS",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,0000662102866002  
                            ", FECHA HEADER FILE/MOVTOS: ",             0000662202867002  
                            POINTER(ALOG)+2 FOR 8,                      0000662302868002  
                            " FECHA CORRECTA: ",                        0000662402869002  
                            FECDIA FOR 8 DIGITS;                        0000662502870002  
          LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                              0000662602871002  
          ACCEPT(TXTMSG);                                               0000662702872002  
          LOGS[LOGN].MYUSE:= 3;                                         0000662802873002  
         READ(LOGS[LOGN][0],75,ALOG);                                   0000662902874002  
        END;                                                            0000663002875002  
        NIV_SUP:=LOGS[LOGN].LASTRECORD;                                 0000663102876002  
     END                                                                0000663202877002  
     ELSE                                                               0000663302878002  
        NIV_SUP:=0;                                                     0000663402879002  
                                                                        0000663502880002  
     NIV_INF:=0;                                                        0000663602881002  
                                                                        0000663702882002  
     WHILE NIV_INF NEQ NIV_SUP DO                                       0000663802883002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000663902884002  
          NUM_REG:=INTEGERT((NIV_SUP - NIV_INF)/2) + NIV_INF;           0000664002885002  
         READ(LOGS[LOGN][NUM_REG],75,ALOG);                             0000664102886002  
                                                                        0000664202887002  
          IF HFUNCION = 99   THEN                                       0000664302888002  
             NIV_SUP := NUM_REG                                         0000664402889002  
          ELSE                                                          0000664502890002  
             NIV_INF := NUM_REG;                                        0000664602891002  
                                                                        0000664702892002  
          IF NIV_SUP - NIV_INF = 1 THEN                                 0000664802893002  
             NIV_SUP := NIV_INF;                                        0000664902894002  
                                                                        0000665002895002  
       END;                                                             0000665102896002  
     NIVLOG:=NIV_SUP;                                                   0000665202897002  
     J := NIVLOG;                                                       0000665302898002  
    READ(LOGS[LOGN][J:=*+1],75,ALOG);                                   0000665402899002  
     WHILE HFUNCION NEQ 99 DO                                           0000665502900002  
      IF READ(LOGS[LOGN][J:=*+1],75,ALOG) THEN                          0000665602901002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000665702902002  
         REG_SIZE := 75;                                                0000665802906102  
         AMPL_REG := 20000;                                             0000665902906202  
         DFAMPLIAFILE(LOGS[LOGN],J,AMPL_REG,A_VACIO,REG_SIZE);          0000666002907002  
         READ(LOGS[LOGN][0]);                                           0000666102908002  
         LAST_RLOG[LOGN]:=LOGS[LOGN].LASTRECORD;                        0000666202909002  
%        REPLACE POINTER(VACIO,4) BY 99 FOR 2 DIGITS;                   0000666302910002  
%        FOR I:= J STEP 1 UNTIL (J+AMPL_REG) DO                         0000666402911002  
%        BEGIN                                                          0000666502912002  
%            WRITE (LOGS[LOGN][I],30,VACIO);                            0000666602913002  
%        END;                                                           0000666702914002  
%        CLOSE(LOGS[LOGN],LOCK);                                        0000666802915002  
%        REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ARCHIVO "             ,                     0000666902916002  
%                          LOGS[LOGN].TITLE       ,                     0000667002917002  
%                          " AMPLIADO DE "        ,                     0000667102918002  
%                          NIVLOG - 1 FOR 8 DIGITS,                     0000667202919002  
%                          " REGISTROS A "        ,                     0000667302920002  
%                          I          FOR 8 DIGITS;                     0000667402921002  
%        LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                               0000667502922002  
       END;                                                             0000667602923002  
   END                                                                  0000667702924002  
   ELSE                                                                 0000667802925002  
   IF NIVBASE > 0 THEN                                                  0000667902926002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000668002927002  
                                                                        0000668102928002  
      REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR : FALTA ARCHIVO ",                       0000668202929002  
                        LOGS[LOGN].TITLE        ,                       0000668302930002  
                        ", FAVOR DE COPIARLO   ";                       0000668402931002  
                                                                        0000668502932002  
      LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                  0000668602933002  
      ACCEPT(TXTMSG);                                                   0000668702934002  
      GO VERIFICA;                                                      0000668802935002  
   END;                                                                 0000668902936002  
 END ARMALOG;                                                           0000669002937002  
                                                                        0000669102938002

       END;                                                             0000680903071002  
   END                                                                  0000681003072002  
   ELSE                                                                 0000681103073002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000681203074002  
     IF LOGS[LOGN].RESIDENT THEN                                        0000681303075002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000681403076002  
       READ(LOGS[LOGN][0],75,ALOG);                                     0000681503077002  
       IF IDFSTADES = 1 THEN                                            0000681603078002

 PROCEDURE L002COMMPOST(E_PARAM,I_TIPO);                                0000708103369002

   PROCEDURE COMMANDPOST;                                               0000708703375002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000708803376002  
     INTEGER LONGITUD, RESIDUO;                                         0000708903377002  
     POINTER P_LEN;                                                     0000709003378002  
     EXCEPTION PROCEDURE LIBERA;                                        0000709103379002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000709203380002  
        IF LOCKSTATUS (LK_COMANDPOST).[47:24] = PROCESSID THEN          0000709303381002  
           UNLOCK(LK_COMANDPOST);                                       0000709403382002  
     END;                                                               0000709503383002  
     LONGITUD := SIZE(E_PARAM);                                         0000709603384002  
     PROCE := IDFTIPPROC(ALOG1);                                        0000709703385002  
     P_LEN := E_PARAM[0];                                               0000709803386002  
     SCAN    P_LEN FOR RESIDUO:LONGITUD UNTIL = 48"00";                 0000709903387002  
     LONGITUD := SIZE(E_PARAM) - RESIDUO;                               0000710003388002  
     REPLACE POINTER(A_CPOST) BY " " FOR (SIZE(A_CPOST)*6);             0000710103389002  
     REPLACE DF_CPOST_EVENTO BY "5308000 ";                             0000710203390002  
     REPLACE DF_CPOST_TIPO   BY I_TIPO FOR 1 DIGITS                     0000710303391002  
                               ," ";                                    0000710403392002  
     REPLACE DF_CPOST_ORIGEN BY "S151/L002 ";                           0000710503393002  
     REPLACE DF_CPOST_CONCEP BY "S",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,              0000710603394002  
                                "/PR",PROCE FOR 2 DIGITS," ";           0000710703395002  
     IF LONGITUD < 62 THEN                                              0000710803396002  
       REPLACE DF_CPOST_TXT BY "[",E_PARAM FOR LONGITUD,"] ",           0000710903397002  
                                "[] [] []"                              0000711003398002  
     ELSE                                                               0000711103399002  
     IF LONGITUD < 124 THEN                                             0000711203400002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000711303401002  
       REPLACE DF_CPOST_TXT BY "[",E_PARAM FOR 62, "] ",                0000711403402002  
                                "[",E_PARAM[62] FOR LONGITUD-62,        0000711503403002  
                                "] ","[] []";                           0000711603404002  
     END                                                                0000711703405002  
     ELSE                                                               0000711803406002  
     IF LONGITUD < 186 THEN                                             0000711903407002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000712003408002  
       REPLACE DF_CPOST_TXT BY "[",E_PARAM FOR 62,"] ",                 0000712103409002  
                                "[",E_PARAM[ 62] FOR 62,"] [",          0000712203410002  
                                E_PARAM[124] FOR LONGITUD-124,          0000712303411002  
                                "] []";                                 0000712403412002  
     END;                                                               0000712503413002  
%    IF NOM_EQUIPO = "ACYPBETA" OR NOM_EQUIPO = "VDMBETA" THEN          0000712603414002  
%    ELSE                                                               0000712703415002  
%    BEGIN                                                              0000712803416002  
        REPLACE MSGLJ BY POINTER(A_CPOST) FOR (SIZE(A_CPOST)*6);        0000712903417002  
%       DISPLAY(MSGLJ);                                                 0000713003418002  
%    END;                                                               0000713103419002  
     DF_DISPLAY("E>050110<",POINTER(A_CPOST) FOR (SIZE(A_CPOST)*6));    0000713203420002  
     IF LLAMO_A_CPOST THEN                                              0000713303421002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000713403422002  
        RSENDAUTO(A_CPOST);                                             0000713503423002  
        DELINKLIBRARY(LIB_OUTBOARD);                                    0000713603424002  
        REPLACE EA_I_CPST[0] BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(EA_I_CPST);             0000713703425002  
     END;                                                               0000713803426002  
     UNLOCK(LK_COMANDPOST);                                             0000713903427002  
   END OF COMMANDPOST;                                                  0000714003428002

%***************                                                        0000716003463002  
 PROCEDURE BAJA;                                                        0000716103464002  
%***************                                                        0000716203465002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000716303466002  
   INTEGER I,J, RESULT_L001, ESTADO, MEZCLA;                            0000716403467002  
                                                                        0000716503468002  
   BOOLEAN BOOL_FIN;                                                    0000716603469002  
   LABEL REGRESA;                                                       0000716703470002  
                                                                        0000716803471002  
   CLOSE (ERRORES1,LOCK);                                               0000716903473002  
   I:= -1;                                                              0000717003474002  
   WHILE I:=*+1 LEQ 9 DO                                                0000717103475002  
         CIERRALOG(I);                                                  0000717203477002  
   REGRESA:                                                             0000717303480002  
   I:= -1;                                                              0000717403482002  
   WHILE I:=*+1 LEQ 9 DO                                                0000717503483002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000717603484002  
      IF STATUS_MIX_P015(I) > 0 THEN                                    0000717703485002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000717803486002  
         ERRORMSG;                                                      0000717903487002  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                             0000718003491002  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY NUM_MIX_P015(I) FOR 7 DIGITS, " HI 4",         0000718103492002  
                           48"00";                                      0000718203493002  
         DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                   0000718303494002  
         WAIT((2));                                                     0000718403495002  
      END;                                                              0000718503496002  
      IF STATUS_MIX_P016(I) > 0 THEN                                    0000718603496012  
      BEGIN                                                             0000718703496022  
         ERRORMSG;                                                      0000718803496032  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                             0000718903496042  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY NUM_MIX_P016(I) FOR 7 DIGITS, " HI 4",         0000719003496052  
                           48"00";                                      0000719103496062  
         DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                   0000719203496072  
         WAIT((2));                                                     0000719303496082  
      END;                                                              0000719403496092  
      IF STATUS_MIX_P025 > 0 THEN                                       0000719503497002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000719603498002  
         ERRORMSG;                                                      0000719703499002  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                             0000719803503002

                           48"00";                                      0000720003505002

         WAIT((2));                                                     0000720203507002  
      END;                                                              0000720303508002  
   END;                                                                 0000720403509002  
   BOOL_FIN := FALSE;                                                   0000720503510002  
   I:= 0            ;                                                   0000720603511002  
   ESTADO := STATUS_MIX_P025    ;                                       0000720703512002  
   MEZCLA := NUM_MIX_P025       ;                                       0000720803513002  
   VALIDA_STATUS(ESTADO, MEZCLA);                                       0000720903514002  
   WHILE (NOT BOOL_FIN) AND (I LEQ 9) DO                                0000721003515002  
      IF STATUS_MIX_P015(I) > 0 OR                                      0000721103516002  
         STATUS_MIX_P016(I) > 0 OR                                      0000721203517002  
         STATUS_MIX_P025    > 0 THEN                                    0000721303518002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000721403519002  
         BOOL_FIN := TRUE;                                              0000721503520002  
         ESTADO := STATUS_MIX_P015(I) ;                                 0000721603521002  
         MEZCLA := NUM_MIX_P015(I)    ;                                 0000721703522002  
         VALIDA_STATUS(ESTADO, MEZCLA);                                 0000721803523002  
         ESTADO := STATUS_MIX_P016(I) ;                                 0000721903524002  
         MEZCLA := NUM_MIX_P016(I)    ;                                 0000722003525002  
         VALIDA_STATUS(ESTADO, MEZCLA);                                 0000722103526002  
      END                                                               0000722203527002  
      ELSE                                                              0000722303528002  
         I := * + 1;                                                    0000722403529002  
                                                                        0000722503530002  
      IF BOOL_FIN THEN GO REGRESA;                                      0000722603531002  
      REPLACE ACONTROLE2 BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(ACONTROLE2);                0000722703531012

                                       SIST_LIB FOR 4 DIGITS,           0000722903531032  
                                       0        FOR 8 DIGITS,           0000723003531042

      RESULT_L001:= B05PROCESOS(ACONTROLE2);                            0000723203531062

%============================================================           0000723503535002  
%       INICIA PROCEDIMIENTO DE CONTROL                     =           0000723603536002  
%============================================================           0000723703537002  
 PROCEDURE PROC_CONTROL;                                                0000723803538002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000723903539002  
   INTEGER EVENTO, NUM_HI;                                              0000724003540002  
   EBCDIC ARRAY DAT_CONTROL[0:11];                                      0000724103541002  
   NUM_HI := 0;                                                         0000724203542002  
   WHILE NUM_HI NEQ 4 DO                                                0000724303543002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000724403544002  
      EVENTO := WAITANDRESET((2), MYSELF.EXCEPTIONEVENT);               0000724503545002  
      CASE EVENTO OF                                                    0000724603546002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000724703547002  
         1    : EVENTO := 0;                                            0000724803548002  
         2    : BEGIN                                                   0000724903549002  
                  NUM_HI := MYSELF.TASKVALUE;                           0000725003550002  
                  REPLACE DAT_CONTROL[0] BY "0" FOR 12;                 0000725103551002  
                  REPLACE DAT_CONTROL[0] BY NUM_HI FOR 4 DIGITS;        0000725203552002  
                  CONTROLES(DAT_CONTROL);                               0000725303553002  
                  RESET(MYSELF.EXCEPTIONEVENT);                         0000725403554002  
                  MYSELF.TASKVALUE := 0;                                0000725503555002  
               END;                                                     0000725603556002  
         ELSE :    ;                                                    0000725703557002  
      END ;                                                             0000725803558002  
   END;                                                                 0000725903559002  
   WHILE MYSELF.LIBRARYUSERS > 0 DO                                     0000726003560002  
         EVENTO := WAITANDRESET((2), MYSELF.EXCEPTIONEVENT);            0000726103561002  
   MYSELF.STATUS := VALUE(GOINGAWAY);                                   0000726203562002  
 END OF PROC_CONTROL;                                                   0000726303563002  
                                                                        0000726403564002

         CONTROLES;                                                     0000727003570002

     RES_LOCK := UNLOCK(CAMBIA_FECHA);                                  0000727403574002

  LLAMO_A_CPOST := TRUE;                                                0000727703577002  
  FREEZE(CONTROL, PROC_CONTROL);                                        0000727803578002  
  BAJA;                                                                 0000727903579002  
 END.                                                                   0000728003580002
